  - `params` (const QueryParams &): Input parameter.
- Return: Query result (always populated; check result.ok and result.error for failure details).
- Details: Execute. On the Nth call where N == hot_threshold, the query is compiled and the result of that first compiled execution is returned. All subsequent calls go through the specialised path. query Parsed query descriptor. schema Current schema snapshot. params Bind parameter values. Query result (always populated; check result.ok and result.error for failure details). query Input parameter. schema Input parameter. params Input parameter. Return value. #### `std::vector< uint64_t > predict_next_accesses(uint64_t current_address, size_t lookahead=8)`
- Source: `include/performance/intelligent_prefetcher.h`:172
- Brief: Predict the next lookahead addresses after current_address.
- Parameters:
  - `current_address` (uint64_t): Input parameter.
  - `lookahead` (size_t): Input parameter.
- Return: Ordered vector of predicted addresses (may be empty when confidence is too low or the history is insufficient).
- Details: Predict next accesses. Uses the learned stride to extrapolate future addresses. Only predictions that exceed the configured confidence threshold are returned. current_address The address from which to project. lookahead Maximum number of future addresses to return. Capped at max_prefetch_distance. Ordered vector of predicted addresses (may be empty when confidence is too low or the history is insufficient). current_address Input parameter. lookahead Input parameter. Return value. Implements predict_next_accesses without additional internal calls.

#### `void prefetch_predicted(const std::vector< uint64_t > &addresses)`
- Source: `include/performance/intelligent_prefetcher.h`:192
- Brief: Issue hardware prefetch instructions for the given addresses.
- Parameters:
  - `addresses` (const std::vector< uint64_t > &): Input parameter.
- Details: Prefetch predicted. Routes each address to the appropriate cache level: L1 when confidence >= 0.9 L2 when confidence >= 0.75 L3 when confidence >= confidence_threshold DRAM (non-temporal) for very low-confidence streaming patterns Increments total_prefetches for each address issued. When enable_hardware_prefetch is false the counters are still updated but no CPU instruction is emitted. addresses Addresses to prefetch (typically from predict_next_accesses()). addresses Input parameter. Implements prefetch_predicted without additional internal calls.

#### `void record_access(uint64_t address, uint64_t timestamp=0)`
- Source: `include/performance/intelligent_prefetcher.h`:157
- Brief: Record a memory access and update the pattern model.
- Parameters:
  - `address` (uint64_t): Input parameter.
  - `timestamp` (uint64_t): Input parameter.
- Details: Record access. This is the primary training input for the learning engine. Each call appends address to the sliding history window and re-fits the dominant stride. When an address matches a prior prediction the useful_prefetches counter is incremented (feedback loop). address 64-bit address of the accessed memory location. timestamp Access timestamp (nanoseconds since epoch, or any monotonic counter). Used to adapt prefetch distance. address Input parameter. timestamp Input parameter. Implements record_access without additional internal calls.

- Details: Returns the lower bound of the bucket that contains the p-th percentile sample. In Linear mode this is bucket_index * bucket_width. In Exponential mode this is 2^(bucket_index-1) (or 0 for bucket 0). p Percentile in the range [0, 100]. Approximate lower-bound value of the percentile bucket, as a double. std::invalid_argument if p is outside [0, 100]. std::runtime_error if the histogram is empty.

Used by Loop 2 (Workload) in ContinuousLearningOrchestrator to decide whether to trigger retraining (threshold: > 0.1). Drift score in [0.0, 1.0]. Returns 0.0 when fewer than two adaptation events have been recorded.

#### `std::unordered_map< std::string, double > getCalibrationFactors(const OptimizerCostModel::CostConstants *current=nullptr) const`
- Source: `include/performance/phase3/per_query_cost_model.h`:179
- Brief: Compute calibration factors from recorded history.
- Parameters:
  - `current` (const OptimizerCostModel::CostConstants *): Optional pointer to the model's current CostConstants. When provided the GPU/msgpack threshold adjustments are computed relative to the actual configured values rather than the compile-time defaults, preventing threshold oscillation after repeated calibrations. Pass nullptr (default) for backwards compatibility.
- Details: Returns a map of OptimizerCostModel constant names to updated values derived from measured CPU and I/O rates. Pass to OptimizerCostModel::calibrateCosts(). Additionally emits: "gpu_row_threshold_low" when GPU-path records show high serialization overhead (serialization_time_ms / execution_time_ms > 0.5), suggesting the GPU breakeven threshold should be raised. "msgpack_row_threshold" when CPU_SINGLE records show the row count distribution warrants switching to binary format at a lower threshold. current Optional pointer to the model's current CostConstants. When provided the GPU/msgpack threshold adjustments are computed relative to the actual configured values rather than the compile-time defaults, preventing threshold oscillation after repeated calibrations. Pass nullptr (default) for backwards compatibility.

- Source: `src/performance/phase4/pmem_storage.cpp`:52
- Brief: Detect pmem devices.
- Parameters: none
- Return: Return value.
- Details: Detects PMem devices available on the current host. On Linux this enumerates /dev/pmem* block devices and checks for DAX-capable mounts in /proc/mounts. On other platforms it always returns an empty list (caller may fall back to file-based emulation via PMemPool). Return value. Calls: fopen(), fscanf(), strstr(), stat(), push_back(), std::move(), fclose().

#### `void pmem_flush(const void *addr) noexcept`
- Source: `include/performance/phase4/pmem_storage.h`:78
- Brief: n/a
- Parameters:
  - `addr` (const void *): n/a
- Details: Flush a single cache line containing addr to the persistence domain. On x86-64 prefers clwb (non-invalidating); falls back to clflushopt or clflush. On other architectures this is a no-op (PMem is not supported).

- Source: `include/performance/phase4/io_uring_zero_copy.h`:199
- Brief: n/a

#### `int send_zerocopy(int fd, uint32_t buf_index, size_t len) noexcept`
- Source: `include/performance/phase4/io_uring_zero_copy.h`:191
- Brief: n/a

  - `fd` (int): n/a
  - `buf_index` (uint32_t): n/a
  - `len` (size_t): n/a
- Return: 0 on success (io_uring submission or fallback), -errno on error.
- Details: Enqueue a zero-copy send of len bytes from pre-registered buffer at index buf_index. The caller must have written len bytes into get_buffer(buf_index).data() before calling this. On io_uring: submits an IORING_OP_SEND with IORING_RECVSEND_FIXED_BUF. On fallback: calls send() immediately and increments fallback_sends. 0 on success (io_uring submission or fallback), -errno on error.

  - `new_value` (T *): New value to set
- Return: Old value (caller must defer reclamation via call_rcu)

- Brief: Defer deletion of an object until after grace period.

  - `ptr` (T *): Pointer to object to delete
